local function playNamed(a1, a2) -- Line: 441; types: a1: table, a2: string
    local rig = a1.rig
    local v1 = rig and rig.tracks[a2]

    if v1 and #v1 ~= 0 then
        local v2 = v1[math.random(#v1)]

        if rig.held then
            if a2 == "draw" or a2 == "release" then
                rig.held:Stop(0.05)

                rig.held = nil
            end
        end

        if a2 ~= "draw" then
            v2:Play(0.08)
            return
        end

        local def = a1.def and a1.def.drawSeconds
        v2:Play(0.08, 1, if not def then 1 else if not (def > 0) then 1 else if not (0 < v2.Length) then 1 else v2.Length / def)

        rig.held = v2
        rig.heldSince = os.clock()
    end
end

local function holdDraw(a1, a2) -- Line: 470; types: a1: table, a2: table
    local held = a2.held

    if not held then
        return
    end

    local drawSeconds = a1.def and a1.def.drawSeconds or 1
    local v1 = os.clock() - (a2.heldSince or 0)

    if drawSeconds + 1.5 < v1 then
        held:Stop(0.2)

        a2.held = nil

        return
    end

    if held.Length <= 0 then
        return
    end

    local v2 = math.max(held.Length - 0.03, 0)

    if not held.IsPlaying then
        held:Play(0, 1, 0)

        held.TimePosition = v2

        return
    end

    if 0 < held.Speed and v2 <= held.TimePosition then
        held:AdjustSpeed(0)

        held.TimePosition = v2
    end
end

local function jellyScale(a1, a2) -- Line: 495; upvalues: Config (val); types: a1: table, a2: number
    local hopAt = a1.hopAt

    if a1.jelly and hopAt then
        local hopSecondsInAir = Config.Enemy.HubGuardians.hopSecondsInAir
        local v1 = a2 - hopAt

        if not (v1 < 0) and not (hopSecondsInAir <= 0) then
            if v1 < hopSecondsInAir then
                return math.sin((math.clamp(v1 / hopSecondsInAir, 0, 1)) * 3.141592653589793)
                    * 0.36
                    + 1
            end

            local v2 = v1 - hopSecondsInAir

            if v2 < 0.16 then
                return 1 - 0.52 * (1 - v2 / 0.16)
            end

            return 1
        end

        return 1
    end

    return 1
end

local function poseAt(a1, a2) -- Line: 516; types: a1: table, a2: number
    local v1, v2
    local snapshots = a1.snapshots
    local v3 = snapshots[1]

    if a2 <= v3.at then
        return v3.cframe
    end

    local v4 = #snapshots

    for i = 2, v4 do
        v3 = snapshots[i]

        if a2 <= v3.at then
            v1 = snapshots[i - 1]
            v2 = v3.at - v1.at

            return v1.cframe:Lerp(v3.cframe, if not (v2 > 0) then 1 else (a2 - v1.at) / v2)
        end
    end

    return snapshots[#snapshots].cframe
end

local function stretched(a1, a2, a3) -- Line: 535; types: a1: table, a2: number, a3: number
    local offset = a1.offset

    local function along(a1) -- Line: 537; upvalues: a3 (val), a2 (val); types: a1: vector
        return (math.sqrt((a1.X * a3) ^ 2 + (a1.Y * a2) ^ 2 + (a1.Z * a3) ^ 2))
    end

    local X = a1.size.X
    local RightVector = offset.RightVector
    local v1 = X
        * math.sqrt((RightVector.X * a3) ^ 2 + (RightVector.Y * a2) ^ 2 + (RightVector.Z * a3) ^ 2)
    local Y = a1.size.Y
    local UpVector = offset.UpVector
    local v2 = Y
        * math.sqrt((UpVector.X * a3) ^ 2 + (UpVector.Y * a2) ^ 2 + (UpVector.Z * a3) ^ 2)
    local Z_3 = a1.size.Z
    local v3 = -offset.LookVector

    return (Vector3.new(v1, v2, Z_3 * (math.sqrt((v3.X * a3) ^ 2 + (v3.Y * a2) ^ 2 + (v3.Z * a3) ^ 2))))
end

local function draw(a1, a2)
    -- Line: 550
    -- upvalues: poseAt (val), u48 (val), u49 (val), holdDraw (val), jellyScale (val), stretched (val)
    -- types: a1: table, a2: number
    local Position, copy_2, offset_2, size, v1
    local v2 = a2 - 0.1
    local v3 = poseAt(a1, v2)
    local rig = a1.rig

    if rig then
        table.insert(u48, rig.root)
        table.insert(u49, v3 * rig.offset)

        if a1.anchor then
            table.insert(u48, a1.anchor)
            table.insert(u49, v3)
        end

        holdDraw(a1, rig)

        v1 = poseAt(a1, v2 - 0.1)
        local Magnitude = Vector3.new(v3.X - v1.X, 0, v3.Z - v1.Z).Magnitude
        v1 = Magnitude > 0.08
        local walk = rig.tracks.walk and rig.tracks.walk[1]

        if v1 == rig.walking then
            if v1 and walk then
                rig.rate = rig.rate
                    + ((math.clamp(Magnitude / 0.1 / rig.pace, 0.05, 3)) - rig.rate) * 0.2
                walk:AdjustSpeed(rig.rate)
            end

            return
        end

        rig.walking = v1

        if not walk then
            return
        end

        if not v1 then
            walk:Stop(0.2)
            return
        end

        rig.rate = math.clamp(Magnitude / 0.1 / rig.pace, 0.05, 3)
        walk:Play(0.2, 1, rig.rate)

        return
    end

    local v4 = jellyScale(a1, v2)
    local at = a1.snapshots[#a1.snapshots].at

    if v4 == 1 and a1.lastScale == 1 then
        local posedAt = a1.posedAt

        if at + 0.1 < posedAt then
            return
        end
    end

    a1.posedAt = a2
    v2 = 1 / math.sqrt(v4)
    local v5 = v3 * CFrame.new(0, (v4 - 1) * a1.hitbox.Size.Y / 2, 0)
    v1 = true

    if v4 == 1 then
        v1 = a1.lastScale ~= 1
    end

    local v6 = nil
    local v7 = nil
    local v8 = a1

    for i, j in a1.pieces, v6, v7 do
        offset_2 = j.offset
        Position = offset_2.Position

        table.insert(u48, j.copy)
        table.insert(u49, v5 * CFrame.new(Position.X * v2, Position.Y * v4, Position.Z * v2) * offset_2.Rotation)

        if v1 then
            copy_2 = j.copy
            size = if v4 ~= 1 then stretched(j, v4, v2) else j.size
            copy_2.Size = size
        end
    end

    local anchor_2 = v8.anchor

    if anchor_2 then
        table.insert(u48, anchor_2)
        table.insert(u49, v3)
    end

    v8.lastScale = v4
end

        0.12,

            Highlight:Destroy()
        end
    )
end

function v1.start()
    -- Line: 889
    -- upvalues: CollectionService (val), track (val), untrack (val), RunService (val), Workspace (val), u32 (val)
    -- upvalues: Config (val), release (val), buildRig (val), draw (val), build (val), u48 (val), u49 (val)
    (CollectionService:GetInstanceAddedSignal("Enemy")):Connect(track)
    ;(CollectionService:GetInstanceRemovedSignal("Enemy")):Connect(untrack)

    for i, j in CollectionService:GetTagged("Enemy") do
        track(j)
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        -- Line: 896
        -- upvalues: Workspace (upval), u32 (upval), untrack (upval), Config (upval), release (upval), buildRig (upval)
        -- upvalues: draw (upval), build (upval), u48 (upval), u49 (upval)
        local CFrame_2, drawStuds, snapshots, v1, v2
        local v3 = os.clock()
        local CurrentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local Position = if not CurrentCamera then nil else CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position
        local v4 = nil
        local v5 = nil

        for i, j in u32, v4, v5 do
            if not i.Parent then
                untrack(i)
            elseif not j.dead then
                CFrame_2 = i.CFrame
                snapshots = j.snapshots
                v2 = snapshots[#snapshots]
                drawStuds = j.def and j.def.drawStuds
                    or Config.Appearance.MotionStuds

                if j.folder then
                    drawStuds = drawStuds * 1.15
                end

                v1 = true

                if Position ~= nil then
                    v1 = (CFrame_2.Position - Position).Magnitude <= drawStuds
                end

                if v1 then
                    if CFrame_2 ~= v2.cframe then
                        if 0.1 < v3 - v2.at then
                            table.insert(
                                snapshots,
                                {at = v3 - 0.016666666666666666, cframe = v2.cframe}
                            )
                        end

                        table.insert(
                            snapshots,
                            {at = v3, cframe = CFrame_2}
                        )

                        if #snapshots > 12 then
                            table.remove(snapshots, 1)
                        end
                    end

                    if j.folder and j.dirty then
                        release(j)
                    end

                    j.dirty = false

                    if j.folder then
                        draw(j, v3)
                    elseif j.rigged then
                        buildRig(j)

                        if j.folder then
                            draw(j, v3)
                        end
                    elseif not (#snapshots < 2) then
                        build(j)

                        if j.folder then
                            draw(j, v3)
                        end
                    end
                else
                    if CFrame_2 ~= v2.cframe then
                        if #snapshots > 1 then

function v1.apply(a1, a2) -- Line: 63; upvalues: LocalPlayer (val); types: a1: table, a2: table

        v2[j.userId] = true
        v1 = a2[j.userId]

        end

local function ago(a1) -- Line: 113; types: a1: number
    local v1 = math.max(0, (os.time()) - a1)

    if v1 < 60 then
        return "just now"
    end

    local v2 = math.floor(v1 / 60)

    if v2 < 60 then
        return (("%*m ago"):format(v2))
    end

    v1 = math.floor(v2 / 60)

    if v1 < 24 then
        return (("%*h ago"):format(v1))
    end

    v2 = math.floor(v1 / 24)

    if v2 < 7 then
        return (("%*d ago"):format(v2))
    end

    return (("%*w ago"):format((math.floor(v2 / 7))))
end

local function buildVignette(a1) -- Line: 445; upvalues: Config (val), u115 (val); types: a1: userdata
    local UIGradient, v1
    local LowHealthVignette = Config.Appearance.LowHealthVignette
    local v2 = Color3.fromRGB(LowHealthVignette.Color.r, LowHealthVignette.Color.g, LowHealthVignette.Color.b)
    local EdgeFraction = LowHealthVignette.EdgeFraction

    for i, j in {
        {
            rotation = 90,
            size = UDim2.fromScale(1, EdgeFraction),
            position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0),
        },
        {
            rotation = -90,
            size = UDim2.fromScale(1, EdgeFraction),
            position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1 - EdgeFraction),
        },
        {
            rotation = 0,
            size = UDim2.fromScale(EdgeFraction, 1),
            position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0),
        },
        {
            rotation = 180,
            size = UDim2.fromScale(EdgeFraction, 1),
            position = UDim2.fromScale(1 - EdgeFraction, 0),
        },
    } do
        v1 = Instance.new("Frame")
        v1.Name = "Vignette"
        v1.Size = j.size
        v1.Position = j.position
        v1.BackgroundColor3 = v2
        v1.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        v1.BorderSizePixel = 0
        v1.ZIndex = 0
        v1.Visible = false
        v1.Parent = a1
        UIGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        UIGradient.Rotation = j.rotation
        UIGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), (NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1))})
        UIGradient.Parent = v1

        table.insert(u115, v1)
    end
end

end

local function gamepadBegan(a1)
    -- Line: 996
    -- upvalues: u368 (val), GuiService (val), PortalMoveController (val), BuildController (val), u178 (val)
    -- upvalues: PanelTabs (val), cycleHotbar (val), MountController (val), u377 (ref), Config (val), backpackKey (val)
    -- upvalues: startDrop (val), Aim (val), closeOpenPanels (val)
    if u368[a1] and GuiService.SelectedObject ~= nil then
        return false
    end

    if PortalMoveController.isActive() and a1 == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2 then
        PortalMoveController.confirm()
        return true
    end

    if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2 then
        if not BuildController.isUiOpen() then
            u178.setActionHeld(true, "gamepad")
        end

        return true
    end

    if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonY then
        if not BuildController.isUiOpen() then
            u178.setPickupHeld(true, "gamepad")
        end

        return true
    end

    if a1 ~= Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1 and a1 ~= Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL1 then
        if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp then
            if not MountController.available() or BuildController.isUiOpen() then
                backpackKey()
            else
                local u46 = os.clock()
                u377 = u46

                task.delay(
                    Config.Animal.Mount.PadHoldSeconds,
                    function() -- Line: 1033; upvalues: u377 (upval), u46 (val), MountController (upval)
                        if u377 == u46 then
                            u377 = nil
                            MountController.toggle()
                        end
                    end
                )
            end

            return true
        end

        if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.DPadDown then
            startDrop()
            return true
        end

        if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft then
            if not PortalMoveController.isActive() then
                BuildController.rotate()
            else
                PortalMoveController.rotate()
            end

            return true
        end

        if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR3 and Aim.isVR() then
            if not PortalMoveController.isActive() then
                BuildController.rotate()
            else
                PortalMoveController.rotate()
            end

            return true
        end

        if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2 then
            if not BuildController.isUiOpen() then
                BuildController.flip()
            end

            return true
        end

        if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL3 then
            u178.toggleSprintButton()
            return true
        end

        if a1 ~= Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB then
            return false
        end

        if PortalMoveController.isActive() then
            PortalMoveController.cancel()
            return true
        end

        closeOpenPanels()

        return true
    end

    local v1 = if a1 ~= Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1 then -1 else 1

    if not BuildController.isUiOpen() or not PanelTabs.step(v1) then
        cycleHotbar(v1)
    end

    return true
end

    if CooldownDays >= 2 then
        return (("%* days"):format((math.floor(CooldownDays + 0.5))))
    end

    local v1 = CooldownDays * 24

    end

        return (("%* minutes"):format((math.floor(v1 + 0.5))))

end

            u9,

                        if Magnitude <= CountLabelStuds then

                            )
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            v3 = #v1

                    v1,

                        return a1.distance < a2.distance

                    k.gui.Adornee = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end)

local function stepGaits(a1)

    -- upvalues: u90 (ref), u88 (val), Players (val), Mount (val), gaitFor (val), u77 (val)
    -- types: a1: number

            v3 = gaitFor(v4)

            if v3 then

                v5 = if not (u77 <= Magnitude) then
                    if not (Magnitude >= 1.5) then "Idle" else "Walk"
                else

                        if not (Magnitude >= 1.5) then "Idle" else "Walk"
                    else
                        "Run"

                if not v3.tracks[v5] then
                    v5 = if not v3.tracks.Walk then
                        "Idle"
                    else
                        if not (Magnitude >= 1.5) then "Idle" else "Walk"
                end

                    if playing and v3.tracks[playing] then
                        v3.tracks[playing]:Stop(0.2)
                    end

                    v3.playing = v5

                    if v1 then
                        v1.Looped = true
                        v1:Play(0.2)
                    end

                    v2 = math.clamp(Magnitude / (if v5 ~= "Run" then Mount.GaitStudsPerSecond else Mount.RunStudsPerSecond), Mount.GaitRate.min, Mount.GaitRate.max)

                    end

        end
    end
end

            isSolidGround = function(a1_2) -- Line: 213; upvalues: a1 (val), Grid (upval), Config (upval)
                local v1 = a1.occupied[Grid.key(Grid.cell(a1_2.x, a1_2.y, a1_2.z))]
                local Attribute = if not v1 then nil else v1:GetAttribute("BlockId")
                v1 = if not Attribute then nil else Config.Build.Blocks[Attribute]
                local v2 = false

                if v1 ~= nil then
                    v2 = false

                    if v1.shape == nil then
                        v2 = not v1.walkThrough
                    end
                end

                return v2
            end,
            spawns = spawnCells(a1.island),
        },
        Config.Hub.PortalMove.SpawnClearanceCells
    )
end

local function keys() -- Line: 223; upvalues: u41 (val)
    if u41.isTouch() then
        return ""
    end

    return "<b>CLICK</b>  Place     <b>R</b>  Rotate     <b>ESC</b>  Cancel"
end

local function update()
    -- Line: 230
    -- upvalues: u75 (ref), u58 (val), u41 (val), Workspace (val), Grid (val), u77 (ref), refusalFor (val)
    -- upvalues: seatGhost (val), u73 (val), u68 (val)
    local v1 = u75

    if not v1 then
        return
    end

    if v1.island.Parent and v1.portal.Parent then
        local v2 = u41.ray()
        local v3 = RaycastParams.new()
        v3.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
        v3.FilterDescendantsInstances = {v1.island}
        local v4 = Workspace:Raycast(v2.Origin, v2.Direction.Unit * 400, v3)
        v3 = nil
        local Instance_2 = if not v4 then nil else v4.Instance

        while Instance_2 do
            if Instance_2 == v1.island then
                break
            end

            v3 = Instance_2:GetAttribute("CellKey")

            if type(v3) == "string" then
                break
            end

            Instance_2 = Instance_2.Parent
        end

        if not (if not v4 then
            nil
        else
            if type(v3) ~= "string" then
                nil
            else
                if not (0.5 < v4.Normal.Y) then nil else Grid.fromKey(v3)) then
            v1.target = nil
            v1.ghost.Parent = nil
            u77.Text = (if not u41.isTouch() then
                "<b>CLICK</b>  Place     <b>R</b>  Rotate     <b>ESC</b>  Cancel"
            else
                "")
                .. (if (if not u41.isTouch() then
                    "<b>CLICK</b>  Place     <b>R</b>  Rotate     <b>ESC</b>  Cancel"
                else
                    "")
                    == "" then
                    ""
                else
                    "     ")
                    .. "Aim at the top of a block"

            return
        end

        v4 = Grid.cell(v2.x, v2.y + 1, v2.z)
        v1.target = v4
        v1.refusal = refusalFor(v1, v4)

        seatGhost(v1, v4)

        v1.ghost.Parent = Workspace
        v4 = if not v1.refusal then u68 else u73
        v1.highlight.FillColor = v4
        v1.highlight.OutlineColor = v4
        u77.Text = if not v1.refusal then
            if (if not u41.isTouch() then
                "<b>CLICK</b>  Place     <b>R</b>  Rotate     <b>ESC</b>  Cancel"
            else
                "")
                == "" then
                "Tap Place to move your portal here"
            else
                if not u41.isTouch() then
                    "<b>CLICK</b>  Place     <b>R</b>  Rotate     <b>ESC</b>  Cancel"
                else
                    ""
        else
            ("<font color=\"#FF626A\">%*</font>"):format(v1.refusal)

        return
    end

    u58.cancel()
end

local function stop() -- Line: 276; upvalues: u75 (ref), u76 (ref), BuildController (val)
    local v1 = u75

    if not v1 then
        return
    end

    u75 = nil

    if v1.connection then
        v1.connection:Disconnect()
    end

    v1.ghost:Destroy()

    u76.Enabled = false
    BuildController.setUiOpen("PortalMove", false)
end

function u58.begin()
    -- Line: 292
    -- upvalues: u75 (ref), LocalPlayer (val), Config (val), findPortal (val), extentsOf (val), makeGhost (val)
    -- upvalues: readOccupied (val), BuildController (val), u76 (ref), RunService (val), update (val)
    if u75 or LocalPlayer:GetAttribute(Config.Monetization.PortalMoverAttribute) ~= true then
        return
    end

    local v1, v2 = findPortal()

    if v1 and v2 then
        local v3
        _, v3 = extentsOf(v2)
        local v4 = v2:GetAttribute("PortalRotation") or 0
        local v5 = makeGhost(v2)
        local Highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        Highlight.FillTransparency = 0.6
        Highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.2
        Highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
        Highlight.Parent = v5
        local v6 = {
            island = v1,
            portal = v2,
            ghost = v5,
            highlight = Highlight,
            size = {x = v3.X, y = v3.Y, z = v3.Z},
            measuredAt = v4,
            rotation = v4,
            occupied = readOccupied(v1, v2),
            bounds = Config.Build.boundsForTier(v1:GetAttribute("ExpansionTier") or 0),
        }
        u75 = v6

        BuildController.setUiOpen("PortalMove", true)

        u76.Enabled = true
        v6.connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(update)
    end
end

function u58.rotate() -- Line: 332; upvalues: u75 (ref)
    local v1 = u75

    if v1 then
        v1.rotation = (v1.rotation + 90) % 360
    end
end

function u58.confirm() -- Line: 340; upvalues: u75 (ref), u63 (val), u76 (ref), BuildController (val)
    local v1 = u75

    if v1 and v1.target then
        if v1.refusal then
            return
        end

        u63:FireServer(v1.target, v1.rotation)

        v1 = u75

        if not v1 then
            return
        end

        u75 = nil

        if v1.connection then
            v1.connection:Disconnect()
        end

        v1.ghost:Destroy()

        u76.Enabled = false
        BuildController.setUiOpen("PortalMove", false)
